Use whenever a queue keeps growing, memory climbs under load, latency degrades as traffic rises, a consumer lags behind a producer, retries pile up, or a UI cannot keep up with incoming events — and also when sizing buffers, choosing a drop policy, or deciding whether to scale. Trigger even when nobody says "backpressure": it usually arrives as "the queue never drains", "we OOM under load", "should I just add more workers?" or "the dashboard freezes". Refuses… ([getloopscale/loopmem](https://github.com/getloopscale/loopmem/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/backpressure-specialist) · 2 copies)

- **backpressured**: Runs a backpressured automated development loop. A bare `/goal`, or any goal-like request that does not mention backpressure, does NOT trigger this loop.
